נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳—׳©׳‘׳• ׳¢׳ ׳›׳‘׳™׳© ׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳—׳“-׳׳¡׳׳•׳׳™ ׳”׳׳•׳‘׳™׳ ׳׳׳—׳¡׳ ׳׳¨׳›׳–׳™ ׳™׳—׳™׳“ ׳©׳ ׳—׳‘׳¨׳”. ׳›׳ ׳¢׳•׳“ ׳™׳© 4 ׳׳©׳׳™׳•׳×, ׳”׳ ׳׳¡׳×׳“׳¨׳•׳×. ׳›׳׳©׳¨ ׳”׳—׳‘׳¨׳” ׳’׳“׳׳” ׳-128 ׳׳©׳׳™׳•׳×, ׳”׳›׳‘׳™׳© ׳ ׳₪׳§׳§ ׳׳—׳׳•׳˜׳™׳ ׳•׳׳£ ׳׳©׳׳™׳× ׳׳™׳ ׳” ׳–׳–׳” ג€“ ׳–׳•׳”׳™ ׳׳¨׳›׳™׳˜׳§׳˜׳•׳¨׳× UMA ׳”׳׳‘׳•׳¡׳¡׳× ׳¢׳ ׳׳₪׳™׳§ ׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳׳©׳•׳×׳£ (Shared Bus). ׳”׳₪׳×׳¨׳•׳ ׳©׳ NUMA ׳”׳•׳ ׳׳‘׳ ׳•׳× ׳¡׳ ׳™׳£ ׳׳—׳¡׳ ׳§׳˜׳ ׳׳™׳“ ׳›׳ ׳×׳—׳ ׳× ׳׳©׳׳™׳•׳× (Local Memory). ׳׳©׳׳™׳× ׳ ׳™׳’׳©׳× ׳׳׳—׳¡׳ ׳”׳׳§׳•׳׳™ ׳©׳׳” ׳‘׳׳”׳™׳¨׳•׳× ׳”׳‘׳–׳§, ׳•׳¨׳§ ׳׳¢׳™׳×׳™׳ ׳¨׳—׳•׳§׳•׳× ׳ ׳•׳¡׳¢׳× ׳׳׳—׳¡׳ ׳©׳ ׳¡׳ ׳™׳£ ׳׳—׳¨ (Remote Access) ׳‘׳›׳‘׳™׳© ׳‘׳™׳ ׳¢׳™׳¨׳•׳ ׳™ ׳׳”׳™׳¨.

נ”¬ ׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳׳™ ׳•׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™ ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜:
  • ׳׳’׳‘׳׳× UMA: ׳›׳ ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳—׳•׳׳§׳™׳ ׳׳₪׳™׳§ ׳₪׳™׳–׳™ ׳™׳—׳™׳“ ׳׳• ׳׳×׳’ ׳׳¨׳›׳–׳™ ׳”׳׳•׳‘׳™׳ ׳׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳”-RAM. ׳¨׳•׳—׳‘ ׳”׳₪׳¡ ׳©׳ ׳”׳׳₪׳™׳§ ׳”׳•׳ ׳׳©׳׳‘ ׳׳•׳’׳‘׳ ($B$). ׳׳ $N$ ׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳“׳•׳¨׳©׳™׳ ׳¨׳•׳—׳‘ ׳₪׳¡ ׳›׳•׳׳ ׳©׳ $N \times b > B$, ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳׳׳×׳™׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳׳—׳–׳•׳¨׳™ ׳¡׳¨׳§ (Bus Contention).
  • ׳׳¨׳›׳™׳˜׳§׳˜׳•׳¨׳× NUMA: ׳׳¨׳—׳‘ ׳”׳›׳×׳•׳‘׳•׳× ׳”׳₪׳™׳–׳™ ׳ ׳©׳׳¨ ׳׳׳•׳—׳“ ׳•׳©׳§׳•׳£ ׳׳—׳׳•׳˜׳™׳ ׳׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳”, ׳׳ ׳”׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳”׳₪׳™׳–׳™ ׳׳₪׳•׳¦׳ ׳׳¦׳׳×׳™׳ (Nodes). ׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳׳§׳•׳׳™ (Local Node) ׳׳×׳‘׳¦׳¢׳× ׳™׳©׳™׳¨׳•׳× ׳“׳¨׳ ׳‘׳§׳¨ ׳”׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳”׳₪׳ ׳™׳׳™ ׳©׳ ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳׳׳ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳™׳” ׳׳׳₪׳™׳§ ׳”׳¨׳׳©׳™.
  • ׳׳§׳“׳ ׳׳™-׳”׳׳—׳™׳“׳•׳× (NUMA Factor): ׳”׳™׳—׳¡ ׳‘׳™׳ ׳–׳׳ ׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳׳§׳•׳׳™ ($T_{local}$) ׳׳–׳׳ ׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳׳¨׳•׳—׳§ ($T_{remote}$). ׳‘׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳•׳× ׳׳•׳“׳¨׳ ׳™׳•׳× ׳”׳™׳—׳¡ ׳¢׳•׳׳“ ׳¢׳ $1:2$ ׳¢׳“ $1:3$, ׳“׳‘׳¨ ׳”׳׳—׳™׳™׳‘ ׳׳× ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳”׳”׳₪׳¢׳׳” ׳׳”׳§׳¦׳•׳× ׳“׳₪׳™ ׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳‘׳¦׳•׳׳× ׳©׳‘׳• ׳¨׳¥ ׳”׳×׳”׳׳™׳.

ג ן¸ ׳׳׳›׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳ ׳₪׳•׳¦׳•׳× ׳•׳׳§׳¨׳™ ׳§׳¦׳”: ׳”׳ ׳—׳” ׳©׳׳¨׳›׳™׳˜׳§׳˜׳•׳¨׳× NUMA ׳”׳™׳ ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳׳‘׳•׳–׳¨׳× (Distributed System) ׳”׳“׳•׳¨׳©׳× ׳”׳¢׳‘׳¨׳× ׳”׳•׳“׳¢׳•׳× (Message Passing). ׳‘׳₪׳•׳¢׳, ׳”׳—׳•׳׳¨׳” ׳׳¡׳₪׳§׳× ׳׳¨׳—׳‘ ׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳׳©׳•׳×׳£ ׳׳׳ (Shared Memory), ׳•׳›׳ ׳×׳”׳׳™׳ ׳™׳›׳•׳ ׳׳§׳¨׳•׳ ׳•׳׳›׳×׳•׳‘ ׳׳›׳ ׳›׳×׳•׳‘׳× ׳‘׳׳׳¦׳¢׳•׳× ׳₪׳§׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳׳¡׳׳‘׳׳™ ׳¨׳’׳™׳׳•׳× (Load/Store).

נ¯ ׳©׳•׳¨׳” ׳×׳—׳×׳•׳ ׳” ׳•׳׳¡׳§׳ ׳” ׳˜׳›׳ ׳™׳×: UMA ׳׳×׳׳™׳׳” ׳׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳•׳× ׳§׳˜׳ ׳•׳× (SMP ׳©׳•׳׳—׳ ׳™), ׳‘׳¢׳•׳“ NUMA ׳”׳™׳ ׳”׳׳¨׳›׳™׳˜׳§׳˜׳•׳¨׳” ׳”׳‘׳׳¢׳“׳™׳× ׳׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳•׳× ׳©׳¨׳×׳™׳ ׳׳¨׳•׳‘׳•׳× ׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳, ׳”׳“׳•׳¨׳©׳× ׳׳•׳“׳¢׳•׳× ׳׳•׳—׳׳˜׳× ׳©׳ ׳”׳׳×׳–׳׳ ׳•׳׳ ׳”׳ ׳”׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳.
׳©׳׳׳” 8.1.2: ׳¢׳§׳‘׳™׳•׳× ׳׳˜׳׳•׳ (Cache Coherence) ׳•׳₪׳¨׳•׳˜׳•׳§׳•׳ MESI ׳‘׳—׳•׳׳¨׳” ג–¼

נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳—׳©׳‘׳• ׳¢׳ ׳׳•׳— ׳׳•׳“׳¢׳•׳× ׳‘׳׳©׳¨׳“. ׳׳›׳ ׳¢׳•׳‘׳“ ׳™׳© ׳₪׳ ׳§׳¡ ׳׳™׳©׳™ ׳©׳‘׳• ׳”׳¢׳×׳™׳§ ׳׳× ׳׳•׳— ׳”׳–׳׳ ׳™׳ ׳©׳ ׳”׳׳₪׳’׳©. ׳׳ ׳”׳׳ ׳”׳ ׳׳©׳ ׳” ׳׳× ׳©׳¢׳× ׳”׳₪׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳‘׳׳•׳— ׳”׳׳¨׳›׳–׳™, ׳”׳¢׳•׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳™׳₪׳¢׳׳• ׳׳₪׳™ ׳”׳₪׳ ׳§׳¡ ׳”׳™׳©׳ ׳©׳׳”׳ ׳•׳™׳₪׳¡׳₪׳¡׳• ׳׳× ׳”׳₪׳’׳™׳©׳”. ׳›׳“׳™ ׳׳₪׳×׳•׳¨ ׳–׳׳×, ׳™׳© ׳›׳¨׳•׳– ׳©׳׳›׳¨׳™׳– ׳‘׳¨׳׳§׳•׳ (Bus Snooping) ׳‘׳›׳ ׳₪׳¢׳ ׳©׳׳×׳‘׳¦׳¢ ׳©׳™׳ ׳•׳™, ׳•׳›׳ ׳”׳¢׳•׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™׳“ ׳׳•׳—׳§׳™׳ ׳‘׳₪׳ ׳§׳¡ ׳©׳׳”׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳©׳•׳¨׳” ׳”׳™׳©׳ ׳” (Invalidate).

נ”¬ ׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳׳™ ׳•׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™ ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜:
  • ׳׳¦׳‘׳™ ׳₪׳¨׳•׳˜׳•׳§׳•׳ MESI: ׳›׳ ׳©׳•׳¨׳× ׳׳˜׳׳•׳ (Cache Line) ׳׳—׳–׳™׳§׳” ׳©׳×׳™ ׳¡׳™׳‘׳™׳•׳× ׳¡׳˜׳˜׳•׳¡ ׳”׳׳’׳“׳™׳¨׳•׳× ׳׳× ׳׳¦׳‘׳”:

    • Exclusive (E): ׳”׳©׳•׳¨׳” ׳§׳™׳™׳׳× ׳¨׳§ ׳‘׳׳˜׳׳•׳ ׳©׳ ׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳–׳”, ׳׳ ׳”׳™׳ ׳–׳”׳” ׳׳—׳׳•׳˜׳™׳ ׳-RAM.
    • Shared (S): ׳”׳©׳•׳¨׳” ׳¢׳©׳•׳™׳” ׳׳”׳™׳׳¦׳ ׳‘׳׳˜׳׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳©׳ ׳׳¡׳₪׳¨ ׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳‘׳׳§׳‘׳™׳ (׳§׳¨׳™׳׳” ׳‘׳׳‘׳“).
    • Invalid (I): ׳”׳©׳•׳¨׳” ׳₪׳¡׳•׳׳” ׳•׳׳™׳ ׳” ׳׳›׳™׳׳” ׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳×׳§׳₪׳™׳.
  • ׳”׳׳–׳ ׳” ׳׳׳₪׳™׳§ (Bus Snooping): ׳‘׳§׳¨׳™ ׳”׳׳˜׳׳•׳ ׳׳׳–׳™׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳׳•׳₪׳ ׳₪׳¡׳™׳‘׳™ ׳׳›׳ ׳”׳×׳¢׳‘׳•׳¨׳” ׳‘׳׳₪׳™׳§. ׳›׳׳©׳¨ ׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳¨׳•׳¦׳” ׳׳›׳×׳•׳‘ ׳׳©׳•׳¨׳” ׳‘׳׳¦׳‘ Shared, ׳”׳•׳ ׳׳©׳“׳¨ ׳׳•׳× ׳₪׳¡׳™׳׳” (Invalidate). ׳©׳׳¨ ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳©׳•׳׳¢׳™׳ ׳–׳׳× ׳•׳׳¢׳‘׳™׳¨׳™׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳©׳•׳¨׳” ׳©׳׳”׳ ׳׳׳¦׳‘ Invalid.

ג ן¸ ׳׳׳›׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳ ׳₪׳•׳¦׳•׳× ׳•׳׳§׳¨׳™ ׳§׳¦׳”: ׳”׳ ׳—׳” ׳©׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳”׳”׳₪׳¢׳׳” ׳¦׳¨׳™׳›׳” ׳׳”׳¨׳™׳¥ ׳§׳•׳“ ׳׳™׳•׳—׳“ ׳‘׳›׳ ׳₪׳¢׳•׳׳× ׳›׳×׳™׳‘׳” ׳׳׳©׳×׳ ׳” ׳׳©׳•׳×׳£ ׳›׳“׳™ ׳׳¡׳ ׳›׳¨׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳׳˜׳׳•׳. ׳׳׳¢׳©׳”, ׳₪׳¨׳•׳˜׳•׳§׳•׳ MESI ׳׳׳•׳׳© ׳›׳•׳׳• ׳‘׳׳¢׳’׳׳™ ׳—׳•׳׳¨׳” ׳™׳™׳¢׳•׳“׳™׳™׳ ׳•׳₪׳•׳¢׳ ׳‘׳׳”׳™׳¨׳•׳× ׳׳—׳–׳•׳¨ ׳©׳¢׳•׳.

נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳“׳׳™׳™׳ ׳• ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳×׳—׳‘׳•׳¨׳” ׳‘׳™׳ ׳©׳›׳•׳ ׳•׳×: ׳׳₪׳™׳§ ׳™׳—׳™׳“ ׳”׳•׳ ׳›׳‘׳™׳© ׳˜׳‘׳¢׳×׳™ ׳™׳—׳™׳“ ׳©׳›׳•׳׳ ׳—׳•׳׳§׳™׳ (׳–׳•׳ ׳׳‘׳ ׳™׳™׳” ׳׳ ׳ ׳¡׳×׳ ׳׳™׳“). ׳׳×׳’ ׳¦׳•׳׳‘ ׳”׳•׳ ׳‘׳ ׳™׳™׳× ׳’׳©׳¨ ׳™׳©׳™׳¨ ׳₪׳¨׳˜׳™ ׳‘׳™׳ ׳›׳ ׳©׳›׳•׳ ׳” ׳׳›׳ ׳©׳›׳•׳ ׳” ׳׳—׳¨׳× ׳‘׳¢׳™׳¨ (׳׳₪׳¡ ׳₪׳§׳§׳™׳, ׳׳ ׳׳—׳™׳¨ ׳”׳—׳•׳׳¨׳™׳ ׳׳¡׳˜׳¨׳•׳ ׳•׳׳™ ׳•׳׳™׳ ׳• ׳׳¢׳©׳™ ׳׳¢׳¨׳™׳ ׳’׳“׳•׳׳•׳×). ׳¨׳©׳× ׳¨׳‘-׳©׳׳‘׳™׳× ׳”׳™׳ ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳׳—׳׳₪׳™׳ ׳׳“׳•׳¨׳’׳× (׳׳—׳׳£ ׳¦׳•׳׳× ׳“׳¨׳›׳™׳ ׳—׳›׳), ׳”׳׳׳–׳ ׳× ׳‘׳™׳ ׳׳—׳™׳¨ ׳”׳‘׳ ׳™׳™׳” ׳׳‘׳™׳ ׳–׳¨׳™׳׳× ׳”׳×׳ ׳•׳¢׳”.

נ”¬ ׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳׳™ ׳•׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™ ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜:
  • ׳׳₪׳™׳§ ׳׳©׳•׳×׳£ (Shared Bus): ׳¡׳™׳‘׳•׳›׳™׳•׳× ׳—׳•׳׳¨׳” $O(1)$ ׳—׳™׳‘׳•׳¨׳™׳. ׳₪׳©׳•׳˜ ׳•׳–׳•׳, ׳׳ ׳׳׳₪׳©׳¨ ׳¨׳§ ׳”׳¢׳‘׳¨׳” ׳׳—׳× ׳‘׳›׳ ׳¨׳’׳¢ ׳ ׳×׳•׳. ׳׳™׳ ׳• ׳¡׳§׳׳‘׳™׳׳™ ׳׳¢׳‘׳¨ ׳-16-32 ׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳.
  • ׳׳×׳’ ׳¦׳•׳׳‘ (Crossbar Switch): ׳׳˜׳¨׳™׳¦׳” ׳׳׳׳” ׳©׳ ׳׳×׳’׳™׳ ׳”׳׳׳₪׳©׳¨׳× ׳-$n$ ׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳׳’׳©׳× ׳-$m$ ׳™׳—׳™׳“׳•׳× ׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳©׳•׳ ׳•׳× ׳‘׳•-׳–׳׳ ׳™׳× ׳׳׳ ׳—׳¡׳™׳׳”. ׳¡׳™׳‘׳•׳›׳™׳•׳× ׳—׳•׳׳¨׳” $O(n \times m)$ ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳׳™׳×׳•׳’. ׳™׳§׳¨ ׳•׳׳•׳¨׳›׳‘ ׳‘׳™׳•׳×׳¨ ׳׳™׳™׳¦׳•׳¨ ׳₪׳™׳–׳™ ׳¢׳‘׳•׳¨ ׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳¨׳‘׳™׳.
  • ׳¨׳©׳× ׳¨׳‘-׳©׳׳‘׳™׳× (Multistage Interconnect / Omega Network): ׳¨׳©׳× ׳©׳ ׳׳×׳’׳™ $2 \times 2$ ׳”׳׳¡׳•׳“׳¨׳™׳ ׳‘׳©׳׳‘׳™׳. ׳׳—׳™׳‘׳•׳¨ $N$ ׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳-$N$ ׳׳•׳“׳•׳׳™ ׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳ ׳“׳¨׳©׳™׳ $\log_2(N)$ ׳©׳׳‘׳™׳, ׳•׳¡׳ ׳”׳›׳ $\frac{N}{2} \log_2(N)$ ׳׳×׳’׳™׳. ׳₪׳©׳¨׳” ׳׳™׳“׳™׳׳׳™׳× ׳‘׳¡׳™׳‘׳•׳›׳™׳•׳× $O(N \log N)$.

ג ן¸ ׳׳׳›׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳ ׳₪׳•׳¦׳•׳× ׳•׳׳§׳¨׳™ ׳§׳¦׳”: ׳”׳ ׳—׳” ׳©׳‘׳¨׳©׳× ׳¨׳‘-׳©׳׳‘׳™׳× ׳׳™׳ ׳—׳¡׳™׳׳•׳× ׳׳¢׳•׳׳. ׳‘׳₪׳•׳¢׳, ׳¢׳׳•׳׳” ׳׳”׳×׳¨׳—׳© ׳—׳¡׳™׳׳× ׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳‘ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳׳™׳× (Path Contention) ׳›׳׳©׳¨ ׳©׳ ׳™ ׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳׳ ׳¡׳™׳ ׳׳¢׳‘׳•׳¨ ׳“׳¨׳ ׳׳•׳×׳• ׳׳×׳’ ׳₪׳ ׳™׳׳™ ׳‘׳׳•׳×׳• ׳”׳©׳׳‘, ׳’׳ ׳׳ ׳™׳¢׳“׳™ ׳”׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳”׳¡׳•׳₪׳™׳™׳ ׳©׳׳”׳ ׳©׳•׳ ׳™׳.

נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳—׳©׳‘׳• ׳¢׳ ׳׳×׳¨ ׳‘׳ ׳™׳™׳” ׳©׳‘׳• ׳™׳© 10 ׳₪׳•׳¢׳׳™׳ (Slaves) ׳•׳׳ ׳”׳ ׳¢׳‘׳•׳“׳” ׳׳—׳“ (Master). ׳¨׳§ ׳׳׳ ׳”׳ ׳”׳¢׳‘׳•׳“׳” ׳׳•׳×׳¨ ׳׳’׳©׳× ׳׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳™׳•׳× ׳”׳‘׳ ׳™׳™׳”, ׳׳”׳–׳׳™׳ ׳—׳•׳׳¨׳™׳ ׳•׳׳—׳×׳•׳ ׳¢׳ ׳׳™׳©׳•׳¨׳™׳. ׳”׳₪׳•׳¢׳׳™׳ ׳™׳›׳•׳׳™׳ ׳¨׳§ ׳׳”׳ ׳™׳— ׳׳‘׳ ׳™׳. ׳›׳ ׳¢׳•׳“ ׳™׳© ׳©׳₪׳¢ ׳׳‘׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳”׳ ׳™׳—, ׳”׳¢׳‘׳•׳“׳” ׳˜׳¡׳”. ׳׳ ׳׳ ׳›׳ ׳₪׳•׳¢׳ ׳׳¡׳™׳™׳ ׳׳”׳ ׳™׳— ׳׳‘׳ ׳” ׳›׳ 2 ׳“׳§׳•׳× ׳•׳ ׳“׳¨׳© ׳׳׳™׳©׳•׳¨ ׳׳ ׳”׳ ׳›׳“׳™ ׳׳§׳‘׳ ׳׳‘׳ ׳” ׳ ׳•׳¡׳₪׳×, ׳ ׳•׳¦׳¨ ׳×׳•׳¨ ׳¢׳ ׳§ ׳׳¦׳ ׳”׳׳ ׳”׳ ׳•׳”׳₪׳•׳¢׳׳™׳ ׳¢׳•׳׳“׳™׳ ׳—׳¡׳¨׳™ ׳׳¢׳©.

  • ׳”׳™׳•׳•׳¦׳¨׳•׳× ׳¦׳•׳•׳׳¨ ׳”׳‘׳§׳‘׳•׳§: ׳‘׳›׳ ׳₪׳¢׳ ׳©׳×׳”׳׳™׳ ׳‘-Slave ׳׳‘׳¦׳¢ ׳§׳¨׳™׳׳× ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× (System Call) ׳›׳’׳•׳ ׳§׳¨׳™׳׳” ׳׳§׳•׳‘׳¥ ׳׳• ׳©׳׳™׳—׳× ׳—׳‘׳™׳׳× ׳¨׳©׳×, ׳”-Slave ׳ ׳¢׳¦׳¨ ׳•׳©׳•׳׳— ׳₪׳¡׳™׳§׳” ׳-Master. ׳׳ ׳™׳© 32 ׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™ Slave ׳”׳׳‘׳¦׳¢׳™׳ ׳₪׳¢׳•׳׳•׳× I/O, ׳”-Master ׳׳’׳™׳¢ ׳-100% ׳ ׳™׳¦׳•׳׳× ׳•׳”׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳§׳•׳₪׳׳×.

ג ן¸ ׳׳׳›׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳ ׳₪׳•׳¦׳•׳× ׳•׳׳§׳¨׳™ ׳§׳¦׳”: ׳”׳ ׳—׳” ׳©׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳”-Master ׳׳™׳ ׳• ׳™׳›׳•׳ ׳׳”׳¨׳™׳¥ ׳×׳•׳›׳ ׳™׳•׳× ׳׳©׳×׳׳©. ׳‘׳׳¦׳‘ ׳©׳‘׳• ׳׳™׳ ׳§׳¨׳™׳׳•׳× ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳×, ׳”-Master ׳™׳›׳•׳ ׳׳”׳¨׳™׳¥ ׳×׳”׳׳™׳›׳™׳ ׳¨׳’׳™׳׳™׳, ׳׳ ׳×׳₪׳§׳™׳“׳• ׳”׳‘׳׳¢׳“׳™ ׳›׳׳™׳‘׳” ׳”׳•׳₪׳ ׳׳•׳×׳• ׳׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳× ׳›׳©׳ ׳™׳—׳™׳“׳” (Single Point of Failure).

נ¯ ׳©׳•׳¨׳” ׳×׳—׳×׳•׳ ׳” ׳•׳׳¡׳§׳ ׳” ׳˜׳›׳ ׳™׳×: ׳׳•׳“׳ Master-Slave ׳”׳×׳׳™׳ ׳׳©׳׳‘׳™ ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳¨ ׳”׳¨׳׳©׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳¨׳™׳‘׳•׳™ ׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳, ׳׳ ׳ ׳₪׳¡׳ ׳׳©׳™׳׳•׳© ׳׳•׳“׳¨׳ ׳™ ׳¢׳§׳‘ ׳—׳•׳¡׳¨ ׳”׳™׳›׳•׳׳× ׳׳”׳¨׳—׳™׳‘ ׳׳× ׳”׳˜׳™׳₪׳•׳ ׳‘׳§׳¨׳™׳׳•׳× ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳×.
׳©׳׳׳” 8.1.2.3: ׳ ׳¢׳™׳׳× ׳׳™׳‘׳” ׳’׳׳•׳‘׳׳™׳× (Big Kernel Lock - BKL) ׳•׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳¨ ׳׳ ׳¢׳™׳׳” ׳¢׳“׳™׳ ׳” (Fine-Grained) ג–¼
נ¯ ׳©׳׳׳× ׳”׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳“׳™׳׳׳”: ׳׳” ׳”׳™׳” ׳׳ ׳’׳ ׳•׳ ׳”-Big Kernel Lock (BKL) ׳‘׳׳™׳ ׳•׳§׳¡, ׳׳“׳•׳¢ ׳”׳•׳ ׳”׳₪׳ ׳׳× ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳”-SMP ׳׳׳¢׳™׳ ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳—׳“-׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳× ׳‘׳–׳׳ ׳¨׳™׳¦׳× ׳”׳׳™׳‘׳”, ׳•׳›׳™׳¦׳“ ׳‘׳•׳¦׳¢ ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳¨ ׳׳ ׳¢׳™׳׳” ׳¢׳“׳™׳ ׳” (Fine-Grained Locking)?

נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳—׳©׳‘׳• ׳¢׳ ׳‘׳ ׳™׳™׳ ׳׳©׳¨׳“׳™׳ ׳¢׳ ׳§ ׳¢׳ 100 ׳—׳“׳¨׳™׳. ׳‘׳׳§׳•׳ ׳׳©׳™׳ ׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳ ׳•׳׳₪׳×׳— ׳ ׳₪׳¨׳“ ׳׳›׳ ׳—׳“׳¨ (׳—׳“׳¨ ׳©׳¨׳×׳™׳, ׳׳¨׳›׳™׳•׳, ׳׳˜׳‘׳—׳•׳), ׳”׳ ׳”׳׳× ׳”׳‘׳ ׳™׳™׳ ׳”׳—׳׳™׳˜׳” ׳¢׳ ׳₪׳×׳¨׳•׳ ׳₪׳©׳•׳˜: ׳׳₪׳×׳— ׳׳׳¡׳˜׳¨ ׳׳—׳“ ׳•׳™׳—׳™׳“ ׳׳›׳ ׳”׳‘׳ ׳™׳™׳ ׳”׳×׳׳•׳™ ׳‘׳›׳ ׳™׳¡׳”. ׳›׳ ׳¢׳•׳‘׳“ ׳©׳¨׳•׳¦׳” ׳׳”׳™׳›׳ ׳¡ ׳׳—׳“׳¨ ׳›׳׳©׳”׳• ׳׳•׳§׳— ׳׳× ׳”׳׳₪׳×׳—, ׳ ׳›׳ ׳¡ ׳•׳ ׳•׳¢׳ ׳׳—׳¨׳™׳• ׳׳× ׳“׳׳× ׳”׳›׳ ׳™׳¡׳” ׳”׳¨׳׳©׳™׳× ׳©׳ ׳”׳‘׳ ׳™׳™׳! ׳›׳ ׳©׳׳¨ 99 ׳”׳¢׳•׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳׳׳×׳™׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳—׳•׳¥ ׳‘׳§׳•׳¨, ׳’׳ ׳׳ ׳”׳ ׳¨׳•׳¦׳™׳ ׳׳’׳©׳× ׳׳—׳“׳¨ ׳¨׳™׳§ ׳׳—׳׳•׳˜׳™׳ ׳‘׳§׳¦׳” ׳”׳©׳ ׳™.

נ”¬ ׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳׳™ ׳•׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™ ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜:
  • BKL ׳‘׳׳™׳ ׳•׳§׳¡ (smp_lock): ׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳ ׳¡׳₪׳™׳ ׳׳•׳§ ׳’׳׳•׳‘׳׳™ ׳™׳—׳™׳“. ׳‘׳›׳ ׳›׳ ׳™׳¡׳” ׳׳׳™׳‘׳” (׳§׳¨׳™׳׳× ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳×, ׳₪׳¡׳™׳§׳”), ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳”׳™׳” ׳×׳•׳₪׳¡ ׳׳× ׳”-BKL ׳•׳׳©׳—׳¨׳¨ ׳׳•׳×׳• ׳¨׳§ ׳‘׳™׳¦׳™׳׳” ׳-User Mode.
  • ׳”׳׳—׳™׳¨ ׳‘׳‘׳™׳¦׳•׳¢׳™׳: ׳¨׳§ ׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳׳—׳“ ׳‘׳›׳ ׳¨׳’׳¢ ׳ ׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳›׳•׳ ׳”׳™׳” ׳׳¨׳•׳¥ ׳‘׳×׳•׳ ׳”׳׳™׳‘׳”. ׳׳ ׳׳¢׳‘׳“ 1 ׳‘׳™׳¦׳¢ ׳₪׳¢׳•׳׳× ׳›׳×׳™׳‘׳” ׳׳“׳™׳¡׳§, ׳׳¢׳‘׳“ 2 ׳׳ ׳™׳›׳•׳ ׳”׳™׳” ׳׳₪׳™׳׳• ׳׳‘׳¦׳¢ ׳§׳¨׳™׳׳× getpid() ׳˜׳¨׳™׳•׳•׳™׳׳׳™׳×.
  • ׳ ׳¢׳™׳׳” ׳¢׳“׳™׳ ׳” (Fine-Grained Locking): ׳₪׳™׳¨׳•׳§ ׳”׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳ ׳”׳’׳׳•׳‘׳׳™ ׳׳׳׳•׳× ׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳׳™׳ ׳ ׳§׳•׳“׳×׳™׳™׳: ׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳ ׳׳›׳ Inode, ׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳ ׳׳×׳•׳¨ ׳”׳×׳–׳׳•׳, ׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳ ׳׳˜׳‘׳׳× ׳”׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳”׳•׳™׳¨׳˜׳•׳׳׳™ (mm_struct). ׳©׳ ׳™ ׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳™׳›׳•׳׳™׳ ׳׳¨׳•׳¥ ׳‘׳׳™׳‘׳” ׳‘׳׳§׳‘׳™׳ ׳›׳ ׳¢׳•׳“ ׳”׳ ׳׳™׳ ׳ ׳ ׳™׳’׳©׳™׳ ׳׳׳•׳×׳• ׳׳•׳‘׳™׳™׳§׳˜.
  • ׳”׳¡׳›׳ ׳”: Deadlocks: ׳¨׳™׳‘׳•׳™ ׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳׳™׳ ׳׳™׳™׳¦׳¨ ׳¡׳›׳ ׳× ׳§׳™׳₪׳׳•׳ ׳”׳“׳“׳™׳×. ׳ ׳“׳¨׳© ׳¡׳“׳¨ ׳ ׳¢׳™׳׳” ׳§׳©׳™׳— (Lock Ordering Hierarchy).

ג ן¸ ׳׳׳›׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳ ׳₪׳•׳¦׳•׳× ׳•׳׳§׳¨׳™ ׳§׳¦׳”: ׳”׳ ׳—׳” ׳©׳ ׳¢׳™׳׳” ׳¢׳“׳™׳ ׳” ׳”׳™׳ ׳×׳׳™׳“ ׳׳”׳™׳¨׳” ׳™׳•׳×׳¨. ׳׳ ׳§׳•׳“ ׳ ׳“׳¨׳© ׳׳×׳₪׳•׳¡ 20 ׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳׳™׳ ׳¢׳“׳™׳ ׳™׳ ׳©׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳–׳” ׳׳—׳¨ ׳–׳” ׳›׳“׳™ ׳׳‘׳¦׳¢ ׳׳©׳™׳׳” ׳§׳¦׳¨׳”, ׳×׳§׳•׳¨׳× ׳”׳ ׳¢׳™׳׳•׳× (Lock Overhead) ׳¢׳׳•׳׳” ׳׳¢׳׳•׳× ׳¢׳ ׳×׳§׳•׳¨׳× ׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳ ׳™׳—׳™׳“.

נ¯ ׳©׳•׳¨׳” ׳×׳—׳×׳•׳ ׳” ׳•׳׳¡׳§׳ ׳” ׳˜׳›׳ ׳™׳×: ׳”׳¡׳¨׳× ׳”-BKL ׳•׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳¨ ׳׳ ׳¢׳™׳׳” ׳¢׳“׳™׳ ׳” (׳©׳”׳•׳©׳׳׳” ׳‘׳׳™׳ ׳•׳§׳¡ 2.6.39) ׳׳₪׳©׳¨׳” ׳׳©׳¨׳×׳™׳ ׳׳•׳“׳¨׳ ׳™׳™׳ ׳׳”׳₪׳™׳§ ׳‘׳™׳¦׳•׳¢׳™׳ ׳׳׳™׳×׳™׳™׳ ׳׳׳׳•׳× ׳׳™׳‘׳•׳× ׳‘׳׳§׳‘׳™׳.
׳©׳׳׳” 8.1.2.4: ׳ ׳™׳”׳•׳ ׳×׳•׳¨׳™ ׳׳•׳›׳ ׳™׳ ׳‘׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× SMP ג€“ ׳×׳•׳¨ ׳™׳—׳™׳“ ׳׳•׳ ׳×׳•׳¨׳™׳ ׳׳‘׳•׳–׳¨׳™׳ ג–¼
נ¯ ׳©׳׳׳× ׳”׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳“׳™׳׳׳”: ׳›׳™׳¦׳“ ׳׳©׳₪׳™׳¢׳” ׳”׳‘׳—׳™׳¨׳” ׳”׳׳¨׳›׳™׳˜׳§׳˜׳•׳ ׳™׳× ׳‘׳™׳ ׳×׳•׳¨ ׳׳•׳›׳ ׳™׳ ׳™׳—׳™׳“ ׳•׳’׳׳•׳‘׳׳™ (Single Global Runqueue) ׳׳‘׳™׳ ׳×׳•׳¨ ׳׳•׳›׳ ׳™׳ ׳ ׳₪׳¨׳“ ׳׳›׳ ׳׳¢׳‘׳“ (Per-CPU Runqueue) ׳¢׳ ׳×׳§׳•׳¨׳× ׳”׳ ׳¢׳™׳׳•׳×, ׳׳™׳–׳•׳ ׳”׳¢׳•׳׳¡׳™׳ ׳•׳–׳™׳§׳× ׳”׳׳˜׳׳•׳?

נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳—׳©׳‘׳• ׳¢׳ ׳×׳•׳¨ ׳‘׳‘׳ ׳§. ׳׳₪׳©׳¨׳•׳× ׳' ׳”׳™׳ ׳×׳•׳¨ ׳™׳—׳™׳“ ׳‘׳›׳ ׳™׳¡׳” ג€“ ׳”׳׳§׳•׳— ׳”׳¨׳׳©׳•׳ ׳”׳•׳׳ ׳׳₪׳§׳™׳“ ׳”׳¨׳׳©׳•׳ ׳©׳׳×׳₪׳ ׳”. ׳”׳™׳×׳¨׳•׳: ׳׳₪׳¡ ׳׳‘׳˜׳׳”, ׳”׳₪׳§׳™׳“׳™׳ ׳×׳׳™׳“ ׳¢׳•׳‘׳“׳™׳ (׳׳™׳–׳•׳ ׳׳•׳©׳׳). ׳”׳—׳™׳¡׳¨׳•׳: ׳׳ ׳™׳© 64 ׳₪׳§׳™׳“׳™׳, ׳›׳•׳׳ ׳¦׳•׳¢׳§׳™׳ ׳™׳—׳“ ׳׳›׳ ׳™׳¡׳” ׳›׳“׳™ ׳׳׳©׳•׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳׳§׳•׳— ׳”׳‘׳ ׳•׳“׳•׳—׳₪׳™׳ ׳–׳” ׳׳× ׳–׳” (׳×׳—׳¨׳•׳× ׳ ׳¢׳™׳׳” ׳¢׳ ׳¨׳׳© ׳”׳×׳•׳¨). ׳׳₪׳©׳¨׳•׳× ׳‘' ׳”׳™׳ ׳×׳•׳¨ ׳ ׳₪׳¨׳“ ׳׳₪׳ ׳™ ׳›׳ ׳׳©׳ ׳‘ ג€“ ׳׳™׳ ׳©׳•׳ ׳“׳—׳™׳₪׳•׳×, ׳׳ ׳׳ ׳₪׳§׳™׳“ ׳׳—׳“ ׳¡׳™׳™׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳×׳•׳¨ ׳©׳׳• ׳”׳•׳ ׳™׳•׳©׳‘ ׳‘׳‘׳˜׳׳” ׳‘׳–׳׳ ׳©׳‘׳׳©׳ ׳‘ ׳©׳׳™׳“׳• ׳”׳×׳•׳¨ ׳׳×׳₪׳×׳ ׳¢׳“ ׳”׳“׳׳×.

נ”¬ ׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳׳™ ׳•׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™ ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜:
  • ׳×׳•׳¨ ׳’׳׳•׳‘׳׳™ ׳™׳—׳™׳“ (Global Queue): ׳׳™׳–׳•׳ ׳¢׳•׳׳¡׳™׳ ׳׳•׳©׳׳ ($O(1)$ ׳׳׳¦׳™׳׳× ׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳₪׳ ׳•׳™), ׳׳ ׳“׳•׳¨׳© ׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳ ׳¡׳₪׳™׳ ׳׳•׳§ ׳’׳׳•׳‘׳׳™. ׳‘׳›׳ ׳₪׳¢׳ ׳©׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳׳×׳₪׳ ׳” ׳׳• ׳©׳×׳”׳׳™׳ ׳׳×׳¢׳•׳¨׳¨, ׳™׳© ׳׳×׳₪׳•׳¡ ׳׳× ׳”׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳. ׳‘׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳•׳× ׳¢׳ 32+ ׳׳™׳‘׳•׳×, ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳׳‘׳׳™׳ ׳׳× ׳¨׳•׳‘ ׳–׳׳ ׳ ׳‘׳”׳׳×׳ ׳” ׳׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳ ׳”׳×׳•׳¨. ׳‘׳ ׳•׳¡׳£, ׳×׳”׳׳™׳ ׳ ׳•׳˜׳” ׳׳ ׳“׳•׳“ ׳›׳ ׳”׳–׳׳ ׳‘׳™׳ ׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳©׳•׳ ׳™׳, ׳׳” ׳©׳”׳•׳¨׳¡ ׳׳× ׳–׳™׳§׳× ׳”׳׳˜׳׳•׳ (Cache Affinity).
  • ׳×׳•׳¨ ׳ ׳₪׳¨׳“ ׳׳›׳ ׳׳¢׳‘׳“ (Per-CPU Runqueue): ׳׳›׳ ׳׳™׳‘׳” ׳™׳© ׳×׳•׳¨ ׳₪׳¨׳˜׳™ ׳•׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳ ׳₪׳¨׳˜׳™. ׳׳×׳–׳׳ ׳”׳׳™׳‘׳” ׳ ׳™׳’׳© ׳׳×׳•׳¨ ׳©׳׳• ׳׳׳ ׳©׳•׳ ׳×׳—׳¨׳•׳× ׳¢׳ ׳׳™׳‘׳•׳× ׳׳—׳¨׳•׳× ($O(1)$ ׳׳׳ ׳ ׳¢׳™׳׳” ׳—׳™׳¦׳•׳ ׳™׳×). ׳×׳”׳׳™׳›׳™׳ ׳ ׳©׳׳¨׳™׳ ׳‘׳׳•׳×׳” ׳׳™׳‘׳” ׳•׳׳ ׳¦׳׳™׳ ׳׳˜׳׳•׳ ׳—׳.
  • ׳׳ ׳’׳ ׳•׳ ׳׳™׳–׳•׳ ׳¢׳•׳׳¡׳™׳ (Load Balancing / Work Stealing): ׳›׳׳©׳¨ ׳׳™׳‘׳” ׳׳–׳”׳” ׳©׳”׳×׳•׳¨ ׳©׳׳” ׳”׳×׳¨׳•׳§׳, ׳”׳™׳ ׳׳₪׳¢׳™׳׳” ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳ "׳’׳ ׳™׳‘׳× ׳¢׳‘׳•׳“׳”" ׳•׳׳•׳©׳›׳× ׳×׳”׳׳™׳›׳™׳ ׳׳”׳§׳¦׳” ׳”׳׳—׳•׳¨׳™ ׳©׳ ׳×׳•׳¨ ׳©׳ ׳׳™׳‘׳” ׳¢׳׳•׳¡׳” ׳׳—׳¨׳×.

נ”¬ ׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳׳™ ׳•׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™ ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜:

נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳—׳©׳‘׳• ׳¢׳ ׳›׳¡׳₪׳× ׳‘׳ ׳§ ׳©׳‘׳” ׳©׳ ׳™ ׳₪׳§׳™׳“׳™׳ ׳¨׳•׳¦׳™׳ ׳׳©׳™׳ ׳—׳‘׳™׳׳× ׳›׳¡׳£. ׳₪׳§׳•׳“׳” ׳¨׳’׳™׳׳” ׳”׳™׳ ׳‘׳©׳ ׳™ ׳©׳׳‘׳™׳: ׳‘׳“׳™׳§׳” ׳”׳׳ ׳”׳›׳¡׳₪׳× ׳₪׳×׳•׳—׳”, ׳•׳׳– ׳”׳ ׳—׳× ׳”׳›׳¡׳£. ׳׳ ׳©׳ ׳™ ׳”׳₪׳§׳™׳“׳™׳ ׳‘׳•׳“׳§׳™׳ ׳‘׳׳•׳×׳• ׳©׳‘׳¨׳™׳¨ ׳©׳ ׳™׳™׳”, ׳©׳ ׳™׳”׳ ׳™׳¨׳׳• ׳©׳”׳™׳ ׳₪׳ ׳•׳™׳” ׳•׳©׳ ׳™׳”׳ ׳™׳ ׳¡׳• ׳׳”׳ ׳™׳— ׳׳× ׳”׳›׳¡׳£. ׳₪׳§׳•׳“׳× TSL ׳‘׳—׳•׳׳¨׳” ׳ ׳•׳¢׳׳× ׳₪׳™׳–׳™׳× ׳׳× ׳‘׳¨׳™׳— ׳”׳›׳¡׳₪׳× ׳×׳•׳ ׳›׳“׳™ ׳‘׳“׳™׳§׳×׳” (׳₪׳¢׳•׳׳× Read-Modify-Write ׳׳˜׳•׳׳™׳× ׳‘׳׳×׳™ ׳ ׳™׳×׳ ׳× ׳׳₪׳™׳¦׳•׳).

נ”¬ ׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳׳™ ׳•׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™ ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜:

  • ׳ ׳¢׳™׳׳× ׳׳₪׳™׳§ ׳”׳—׳•׳׳¨׳” (Bus Locking): ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳׳¨׳™׳ ׳§׳• ׳‘׳§׳¨׳” ׳₪׳™׳–׳™ ׳™׳™׳¢׳•׳“׳™ ׳‘׳׳₪׳™׳§ ׳”׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ (׳׳•׳× #LOCK ׳‘׳׳¨׳›׳™׳˜׳§׳˜׳•׳¨׳× x86). ׳׳׳•׳¨׳ ׳›׳ ׳‘׳™׳¦׳•׳¢ ׳”׳₪׳§׳•׳“׳”, ׳‘׳§׳¨ ׳”׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳—׳•׳¡׳ ׳›׳ ׳‘׳§׳©׳× ׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳׳›׳ ׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳׳—׳¨.
  • ׳ ׳¢׳™׳׳× ׳׳˜׳׳•׳ ׳׳•׳“׳¨׳ ׳™׳× (Cache Lock): ׳‘׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳׳•׳“׳¨׳ ׳™׳™׳, ׳‘׳׳§׳•׳ ׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳ ׳׳× ׳›׳ ׳׳₪׳™׳§ ׳”׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ (׳₪׳¢׳•׳׳” ׳׳™׳˜׳™׳× ׳©׳׳©׳×׳§׳× ׳׳× ׳›׳ ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳), ׳”׳—׳•׳׳¨׳” ׳׳ ׳¦׳׳× ׳׳× ׳₪׳¨׳•׳˜׳•׳§׳•׳ MESI ׳•׳ ׳•׳¢׳׳× ׳׳ ׳•׳¨׳§ ׳׳× ׳©׳•׳¨׳× ׳”׳׳˜׳׳•׳ ׳”׳¡׳₪׳¦׳™׳₪׳™׳× ׳‘׳׳¦׳‘ Exclusive/Modified.

ג ן¸ ׳׳׳›׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳ ׳₪׳•׳¦׳•׳× ׳•׳׳§׳¨׳™ ׳§׳¦׳”: ׳”׳ ׳—׳” ׳©׳₪׳§׳•׳“׳× ׳׳¡׳׳‘׳׳™ ׳‘׳•׳“׳“׳× ׳›׳’׳•׳ inc [var] ׳”׳™׳ ׳׳˜׳•׳׳™׳× ׳‘׳׳•׳₪׳ ׳׳•׳˜׳•׳׳˜׳™ ׳‘׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳׳¨׳•׳‘׳× ׳׳™׳‘׳•׳×. ׳׳׳ ׳§׳™׳“׳•׳׳× LOCK ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳©׳× (׳›׳’׳•׳ LOCK INC [var]), ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳™׳‘׳¦׳¢ ׳§׳¨׳™׳׳” ׳•׳›׳×׳™׳‘׳” ׳ ׳₪׳¨׳“׳•׳×, ׳׳” ׳©׳™׳•׳‘׳™׳ ׳-Race Condition ׳§׳׳׳¡׳™ ׳‘׳™׳ ׳”׳׳™׳‘׳•׳×!

ג ן¸ ׳׳׳›׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳ ׳₪׳•׳¦׳•׳× ׳•׳׳§׳¨׳™ ׳§׳¦׳”: ׳”׳™׳₪׳•׳ ׳¢׳“׳™׳₪׳•׳™׳•׳× ׳©׳¨׳©׳¨׳×׳™ (Chained Priority Inversion): ׳›׳׳©׳¨ ׳×׳”׳׳™׳ ׳×׳•׳₪׳¡ ׳׳¡׳₪׳¨ ׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳׳™׳ ׳‘׳׳§׳‘׳™׳, ׳”׳¢׳׳׳× ׳”׳¢׳“׳™׳₪׳•׳× ׳—׳™׳™׳‘׳× ׳׳—׳׳—׳ ׳׳׳•׳¨׳ ׳›׳ ׳©׳¨׳©׳¨׳× ׳”׳×׳׳•׳× ׳©׳ ׳”׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳׳™׳ (Transitive Inheritance).

נ¯ ׳©׳•׳¨׳” ׳×׳—׳×׳•׳ ׳” ׳•׳׳¡׳§׳ ׳” ׳˜׳›׳ ׳™׳×: ׳›׳ ׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳–׳׳ ׳׳׳× ׳׳• ׳׳™׳‘׳× SMP ׳”׳׳©׳×׳׳©׳× ׳‘׳¢׳“׳™׳₪׳•׳™׳•׳× ׳—׳™׳™׳‘׳× ׳׳×׳׳•׳ ׳‘׳₪׳¨׳•׳˜׳•׳§׳•׳ ׳”׳•׳¨׳©׳× ׳¢׳“׳™׳₪׳•׳™׳•׳× ׳׳׳ ׳™׳¢׳× ׳”׳¨׳¢׳‘׳” ׳©׳ ׳׳©׳™׳׳•׳× ׳§׳¨׳™׳˜׳™׳•׳×.

נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳—׳©׳‘׳• ׳¢׳ ׳¢׳™׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳•׳׳™. ׳׳™׳׳™׳•׳ ׳™ ׳§׳•׳¨׳׳™׳ ׳§׳•׳¨׳׳™׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳’׳™׳׳™׳•׳ ׳©׳ ׳”׳‘׳•׳§׳¨ ׳‘׳•-׳–׳׳ ׳™׳× ׳׳‘׳׳™ ׳׳”׳₪׳¨׳™׳¢ ׳–׳” ׳׳–׳”. ׳׳ ׳”׳¢׳•׳¨׳ ׳¨׳•׳¦׳” ׳׳¢׳“׳›׳ ׳›׳×׳‘׳”, ׳”׳•׳ ׳׳™׳ ׳• ׳¨׳¥ ׳׳›׳ ׳”׳§׳•׳¨׳׳™׳ ׳•׳—׳•׳˜׳£ ׳׳”׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳¢׳™׳×׳•׳ ׳׳”׳™׳“׳™׳™׳. ׳‘׳׳§׳•׳ ׳–׳׳×, ׳”׳¢׳•׳¨׳ ׳׳©׳›׳₪׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳›׳×׳‘׳” ׳‘׳׳—׳©׳‘, ׳¢׳•׳¨׳ ׳׳•׳×׳”, ׳•׳׳“׳₪׳™׳¡ ׳׳”׳“׳•׳¨׳” ׳—׳“׳©׳”. ׳§׳•׳¨׳׳™׳ ׳—׳“׳©׳™׳ ׳™׳§׳‘׳׳• ׳׳× ׳”׳׳”׳“׳•׳¨׳” ׳”׳—׳“׳©׳”. ׳׳” ׳§׳•׳¨׳” ׳¢׳ ׳”׳׳”׳“׳•׳¨׳” ׳”׳™׳©׳ ׳”? ׳”׳¢׳•׳¨׳ ׳׳׳×׳™׳ ׳‘׳¡׳‘׳׳ ׳•׳× ׳¢׳“ ׳©׳›׳ ׳”׳§׳•׳¨׳׳™׳ ׳©׳›׳‘׳¨ ׳§׳¨׳׳• ׳׳× ׳׳”׳“׳•׳¨׳× ׳”׳‘׳•׳§׳¨ ׳™׳¡׳™׳™׳׳• ׳׳§׳¨׳•׳ (Grace Period), ׳•׳¨׳§ ׳׳– ׳–׳•׳¨׳§ ׳׳× ׳”׳¢׳™׳×׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳”׳™׳©׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳׳—׳–׳•׳¨.

נ”¬ ׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳׳™ ׳•׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™ ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜:

  • ׳¢׳‘׳•׳¨ ׳”׳›׳•׳×׳‘ (Updater): ׳”׳›׳•׳×׳‘ ׳׳©׳›׳₪׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳׳•׳‘׳™׳™׳§׳˜ ׳‘׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳, ׳׳¢׳“׳›׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳©׳“׳•׳× ׳‘׳¢׳•׳×׳§ ׳”׳—׳“׳©, ׳•׳‘׳₪׳¢׳•׳׳” ׳׳˜׳•׳׳™׳× ׳׳—׳× ׳©׳ ׳”׳—׳׳₪׳× ׳׳¦׳‘׳™׳¢ (Pointer Swap) ׳׳₪׳ ׳” ׳׳× ׳”׳׳¦׳‘׳™׳¢ ׳”׳¨׳׳©׳™ ׳׳¢׳•׳×׳§ ׳”׳—׳“׳©.
  • ׳×׳§׳•׳₪׳× ׳”׳—׳¡׳“ (Grace Period): ׳”׳›׳•׳×׳‘ ׳׳™׳ ׳• ׳™׳›׳•׳ ׳׳©׳—׳¨׳¨ ׳׳× ׳”׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳©׳ ׳”׳¢׳•׳×׳§ ׳”׳™׳©׳ ׳׳™׳“, ׳›׳™ ׳™׳© ׳§׳•׳¨׳׳™׳ ׳©׳¢׳“׳™׳™׳ ׳׳•׳—׳–׳™׳ ׳‘׳׳¦׳‘׳™׳¢ ׳”׳™׳©׳. ׳”׳›׳•׳×׳‘ ׳׳׳×׳™׳ ׳¢׳“ ׳©׳›׳ ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳‘׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳—׳•׳• ׳׳₪׳—׳•׳× ׳”׳—׳׳₪׳× ׳”׳§׳©׳¨ ׳׳—׳× (Quiescent State). ׳‘׳¨׳’׳¢ ׳–׳” ׳׳•׳‘׳˜׳— ׳©׳›׳ ׳”׳§׳•׳¨׳׳™׳ ׳”׳™׳©׳ ׳™׳ ׳¡׳™׳™׳׳•, ׳•׳”׳›׳•׳×׳‘ ׳׳©׳—׳¨׳¨ ׳׳× ׳”׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳‘׳‘׳˜׳—׳”.

ג ן¸ ׳׳׳›׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳ ׳₪׳•׳¦׳•׳× ׳•׳׳§׳¨׳™ ׳§׳¦׳”: ׳”׳ ׳—׳” ׳©-RCU ׳׳×׳׳™׳ ׳׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳•׳× ׳©׳‘׳”׳ ׳™׳© ׳¢׳•׳׳¡ ׳›׳×׳™׳‘׳•׳× ׳’׳‘׳•׳”. נ¯ ׳©׳•׳¨׳” ׳×׳—׳×׳•׳ ׳” ׳•׳׳¡׳§׳ ׳” ׳˜׳›׳ ׳™׳×: RCU ׳”׳•׳ ׳׳—׳“ ׳”׳—׳™׳“׳•׳©׳™׳ ׳”׳׳‘׳¨׳™׳§׳™׳ ׳‘׳™׳•׳×׳¨ ׳‘׳׳™׳ ׳•׳§׳¡, ׳”׳׳׳₪׳©׳¨ ׳‘׳™׳¦׳•׳¢׳™ ׳§׳¨׳™׳׳” ׳¡׳§׳׳‘׳™׳׳™׳™׳ ׳׳—׳׳•׳˜׳™׳ ׳׳׳ ׳©׳•׳ ׳×׳§׳•׳¨׳× ׳¡׳ ׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳—׳•׳׳¨׳×׳™׳×.

׳©׳׳׳” 8.1.4.1: ׳–׳™׳§׳× ׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳•׳׳˜׳׳•׳ (Processor & Cache Affinity) ׳•׳ ׳“׳™׳“׳× ׳×׳”׳׳™׳›׳™׳ ג–¼

נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳—׳©׳‘׳• ׳¢׳ ׳¡׳˜׳•׳“׳ ׳˜ ׳©׳™׳•׳©׳‘ ׳‘׳¡׳₪׳¨׳™׳™׳” ׳׳™׳“ ׳©׳•׳׳—׳ ׳׳¡' 1: ׳›׳ ׳”׳¡׳₪׳¨׳™׳, ׳”׳׳—׳‘׳¨׳•׳×, ׳”׳׳¨׳§׳¨׳™׳ ׳•׳”׳׳—׳©׳‘ ׳©׳׳• ׳₪׳×׳•׳—׳™׳ ׳•׳׳¡׳•׳“׳¨׳™׳ ׳׳₪׳ ׳™׳• (׳׳˜׳׳•׳ ׳—׳). ׳”׳¡׳˜׳•׳“׳ ׳˜ ׳™׳•׳¦׳ ׳׳”׳₪׳¡׳§׳× ׳׳™׳ ׳§׳¦׳¨׳” ׳©׳ 5 ׳“׳§׳•׳×. ׳׳ ׳›׳©׳™׳—׳–׳•׳¨ ׳”׳¡׳₪׳¨׳ ׳™׳¢׳‘׳™׳¨ ׳׳•׳×׳• ׳׳©׳•׳׳—׳ ׳׳¡' 8 ׳‘׳§׳•׳׳” ׳”׳©׳ ׳™׳™׳” (׳ ׳“׳™׳“׳× ׳×׳”׳׳™׳), ׳”׳¡׳˜׳•׳“׳ ׳˜ ׳™׳‘׳–׳‘׳– ׳—׳¦׳™ ׳©׳¢׳” ׳¨׳§ ׳¢׳ ׳”׳¢׳‘׳¨׳× ׳”׳¦׳™׳•׳“, ׳¡׳™׳“׳•׳¨ ׳”׳¡׳₪׳¨׳™׳ ׳׳—׳“׳© ׳•׳׳¦׳™׳׳× ׳”׳¢׳׳•׳“ ׳”׳ ׳›׳•׳ (׳׳˜׳׳•׳ ׳§׳¨). ׳”׳’׳™׳•׳ ׳™ ׳׳”׳¢׳‘׳™׳¨ ׳׳•׳×׳• ׳׳©׳•׳׳—׳ ׳׳—׳¨ ׳¨׳§ ׳׳ ׳”׳©׳•׳׳—׳ ׳”׳¨׳׳©׳•׳ ׳¢׳•׳׳” ׳‘׳׳© ׳׳• ׳©׳׳™׳©׳”׳• ׳׳—׳¨ ׳—׳™׳™׳‘ ׳׳•׳×׳• ׳‘׳“׳—׳™׳₪׳•׳× ׳¢׳׳™׳•׳ ׳”.

    • ׳”׳׳×׳–׳׳ ׳׳‘׳˜׳™׳— ׳©׳›׳ ׳×׳”׳׳™׳›׳•׳ ׳™ ׳”׳›׳ ׳•׳₪׳™׳” ׳׳×׳—׳™׳׳™׳ ׳׳× ׳₪׳¨׳•׳¡׳× ׳”׳–׳׳ ׳©׳׳”׳ ׳™׳—׳“ ׳¢׳ ׳₪׳ ׳™ ׳§׳‘׳•׳¦׳× ׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳©׳•׳ ׳™׳, ׳•׳׳¡׳™׳™׳׳™׳ ׳׳•׳×׳” ׳™׳—׳“.

  • ׳”׳×׳•׳¦׳׳”: ׳›׳׳©׳¨ $T_2$ ׳׳׳×׳™׳ ׳‘׳¡׳₪׳™׳ ׳׳•׳§ ׳׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳ ׳©׳ $T_1$, ׳”׳•׳ ׳™׳׳×׳™׳ ׳׳›׳ ׳”׳™׳•׳×׳¨ ׳›׳׳” ׳¢׳©׳¨׳•׳× ׳ ׳ ׳•-׳©׳ ׳™׳•׳×, ׳׳›׳™׳•׳•׳ ׳©-$T_1$ ׳¨׳¥ ׳›׳¨׳’׳¢ ׳‘׳׳§׳‘׳™׳ ׳‘׳׳™׳‘׳” ׳”׳¡׳׳•׳›׳” ׳•׳׳©׳—׳¨׳¨ ׳׳× ׳”׳׳ ׳¢׳•׳ ׳׳™׳™׳“׳™׳×.

ג ן¸ ׳׳׳›׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳ ׳₪׳•׳¦׳•׳× ׳•׳׳§׳¨׳™ ׳§׳¦׳”: ׳”׳ ׳—׳” ׳©׳§׳ ׳׳׳׳© Gang Scheduling ׳‘׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× ׳¢׳׳•׳¡׳”. ׳׳ ׳׳©׳™׳׳” ׳“׳•׳¨׳©׳× ׳›׳ ׳•׳₪׳™׳” ׳©׳ 16 ׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳•׳›׳¨׳’׳¢ ׳™׳© ׳¨׳§ 14 ׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳₪׳ ׳•׳™׳™׳, ׳›׳ 14 ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳™׳™׳©׳׳¨׳• ׳¨׳™׳§׳™׳ ׳׳• ׳©׳”׳׳©׳™׳׳” ׳×׳¢׳•׳›׳‘ ׳¢׳“ ׳©׳›׳ 16 ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳™׳×׳₪׳ ׳• ׳‘׳•-׳–׳׳ ׳™׳×.

נ¯ ׳©׳•׳¨׳” ׳×׳—׳×׳•׳ ׳” ׳•׳׳¡׳§׳ ׳” ׳˜׳›׳ ׳™׳×: Gang Scheduling ׳”׳•׳ ׳”׳₪׳×׳¨׳•׳ ׳”׳׳•׳©׳׳ ׳׳™׳™׳©׳•׳׳™׳ ׳׳§׳‘׳™׳׳™׳™׳ ׳‘׳¢׳׳™ ׳×׳§׳©׳•׳¨׳× ׳₪׳ ׳™׳׳™׳× ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳ ׳¡׳™׳‘׳™׳× (Fine-grained Parallelism), ׳”׳׳•׳ ׳¢ ׳©׳¨׳™׳₪׳× ׳׳—׳–׳•׳¨׳™ ׳¡׳₪׳™׳ ׳™׳ ׳’ ׳¡׳¨׳§.
׳©׳׳׳” 8.1.4.4: ׳×׳–׳׳•׳ ׳׳•׳“׳¢ NUMA (NUMA-Aware Scheduling) ׳•׳׳ ׳’׳ ׳•׳ AutoNUMA ג–¼
נ¯ ׳©׳׳׳× ׳”׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳“׳™׳׳׳”: ׳‘׳׳¢׳¨׳›׳× NUMA ׳׳¨׳•׳‘׳× ׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳, ׳›׳™׳¦׳“ ׳׳•׳ ׳¢ ׳”׳׳×׳–׳׳ ׳׳¦׳‘ ׳©׳‘׳• ׳×׳”׳׳™׳ ׳¨׳¥ ׳‘׳¦׳•׳׳× ׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ A ׳׳ ׳›׳ ׳“׳₪׳™ ׳”׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳©׳׳• ׳׳•׳§׳¦׳™׳ ׳‘׳¦׳•׳׳× B, ׳•׳›׳™׳¦׳“ ׳׳ ׳’׳ ׳•׳ AutoNUMA ׳׳‘׳¦׳¢ ׳ ׳“׳™׳“׳× ׳“׳₪׳™׳ ׳“׳™׳ ׳׳™׳×?

נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳—׳©׳‘׳• ׳¢׳ ׳¢׳•׳‘׳“ ׳©׳”׳•׳¢׳‘׳¨ ׳׳׳©׳¨׳“ ׳‘׳¡׳ ׳™׳£ ׳—׳™׳₪׳”, ׳׳ ׳›׳ ׳”׳§׳׳¡׳¨׳™׳ ׳•׳׳¡׳׳›׳™ ׳”׳¢׳‘׳•׳“׳” ׳©׳׳• ׳ ׳©׳׳¨׳• ׳‘׳׳¨׳•׳ ׳‘׳¡׳ ׳™׳£ ׳‘׳׳¨ ׳©׳‘׳¢. ׳‘׳›׳ ׳₪׳¢׳ ׳©׳”׳¢׳•׳‘׳“ ׳¦׳¨׳™׳ ׳׳”׳‘׳™׳˜ ׳‘׳׳¡׳׳, ׳”׳•׳ ׳ ׳׳׳¥ ׳׳ ׳¡׳•׳¢ ׳‘׳¨׳›׳‘׳× ׳׳‘׳׳¨ ׳©׳‘׳¢ ׳•׳׳—׳–׳•׳¨ (׳¢׳•׳ ׳© ׳”׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳”׳׳¨׳•׳—׳§׳× ׳‘-NUMA). ׳”׳₪׳×׳¨׳•׳ ׳”׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳׳™׳’׳ ׳˜׳™ ׳©׳ AutoNUMA ׳”׳•׳ ׳׳–׳”׳•׳× ׳׳׳™׳׳• ׳§׳׳¡׳¨׳™׳ ׳”׳¢׳•׳‘׳“ ׳ ׳™׳’׳© ׳”׳›׳™ ׳”׳¨׳‘׳”, ׳•׳׳”׳¢׳‘׳™׳¨ ׳‘׳”׳“׳¨׳’׳” ׳׳× ׳”׳§׳׳¡׳¨׳™׳ ׳”׳׳׳” ׳‘׳¨׳›׳‘׳× ׳׳©׳ ׳™׳©׳™׳¨׳•׳× ׳׳׳©׳¨׳“׳• ׳”׳—׳“׳© ׳‘׳—׳™׳₪׳”.

נ”¬ ׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳׳™ ׳•׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™ ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜:
  • ׳¢׳•׳ ׳© ׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳׳¨׳•׳—׳§׳× (Remote Access Penalty): ׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳׳§׳•׳׳™ ׳‘׳¦׳•׳׳× ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳׳•׳¨׳›׳× ׳›-60ns, ׳‘׳¢׳•׳“ ׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳©׳ ׳¦׳•׳׳× ׳©׳›׳ ׳“׳¨׳ ׳”׳׳₪׳™׳§ ׳”׳‘׳™׳ ׳¢׳™׳¨׳•׳ ׳™ (UPI) ׳׳•׳¨׳›׳× 150-200ns ׳•׳׳™׳™׳¦׳¨׳× ׳¢׳•׳׳¡ ׳¢׳ ׳׳—׳‘׳¨׳™ ׳”׳—׳•׳׳¨׳”.
  • ׳׳“׳™׳ ׳™׳•׳× ׳”׳”׳§׳¦׳׳” ׳”׳¨׳׳©׳•׳ ׳™׳× (First-Touch Allocation): ׳›׳‘׳¨׳™׳¨׳× ׳׳—׳“׳, ׳”׳׳™׳‘׳” ׳׳§׳¦׳” ׳“׳£ ׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳‘׳¦׳•׳׳× ׳”-NUMA ׳©׳‘׳• ׳¨׳¥ ׳”׳×׳”׳׳™׳ ׳‘׳¨׳’׳¢ ׳©׳‘׳™׳¦׳¢ ׳׳× ׳”׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳”׳¨׳׳©׳•׳ ׳” ׳׳“׳£ (Page Fault ׳¨׳׳©׳•׳).
  • ׳׳ ׳’׳ ׳•׳ AutoNUMA ׳‘׳׳™׳ ׳•׳§׳¡:
    • ׳”׳׳™׳‘׳” ׳׳¡׳׳ ׳× ׳“׳₪׳™ ׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳‘׳׳•׳₪׳ ׳×׳§׳•׳₪׳×׳™ ׳›׳׳-׳ ׳’׳™׳©׳™׳ (PROT_NONE) ׳‘׳׳•׳₪׳ ׳׳׳׳›׳•׳×׳™.
    • ׳›׳׳©׳¨ ׳”׳×׳”׳׳™׳ ׳ ׳™׳’׳© ׳׳“׳£, ׳ ׳•׳¦׳¨ Page Fault ׳׳™׳•׳—׳“ (NUMA Hinting Fault).
    • ׳”׳׳™׳‘׳” ׳‘׳•׳“׳§׳×: ׳׳׳™׳–׳” ׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳”׳’׳™׳¢׳” ׳”׳‘׳§׳©׳” ׳•׳”׳™׳›׳ ׳”׳“׳£ ׳™׳•׳©׳‘ ׳›׳¨׳’׳¢ ׳₪׳™׳–׳™׳×.
    • ׳׳ ׳”׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳׳’׳™׳¢׳” ׳©׳•׳‘ ׳•׳©׳•׳‘ ׳׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳׳¨׳•׳—׳§, ׳”׳׳™׳‘׳” ׳׳₪׳¢׳™׳׳” ׳ ׳“׳™׳“׳× ׳“׳£ (Page Migration) ׳•׳׳¢׳‘׳™׳¨׳” ׳׳•׳×׳• ׳׳¦׳•׳׳× ׳”׳׳§׳•׳׳™ ׳©׳ ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳“.

ג ן¸ ׳׳׳›׳•׳“׳•׳× ׳ ׳₪׳•׳¦׳•׳× ׳•׳׳§׳¨׳™ ׳§׳¦׳”: ׳ ׳“׳™׳“׳× ׳“׳₪׳™׳ ׳‘׳׳×׳™ ׳₪׳•׳¡׳§׳× ׳©׳ ׳׳©׳×׳ ׳™׳ ׳׳©׳•׳×׳₪׳™׳ (Ping-Ponging Pages): ׳›׳׳©׳¨ ׳©׳ ׳™ ׳×׳”׳׳™׳›׳™׳ ׳‘׳¦׳׳×׳™׳ ׳©׳•׳ ׳™׳ ׳›׳•׳×׳‘׳™׳ ׳׳׳•׳×׳• ׳“׳£, AutoNUMA ׳¢׳׳•׳ ׳׳”׳¢׳‘׳™׳¨ ׳׳× ׳”׳“׳£ ׳”׳׳•׳ ׳•׳—׳–׳•׳¨ ׳׳׳ ׳”׳₪׳¡׳§׳”. ׳”׳׳™׳‘׳” ׳›׳•׳׳׳× ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳ ׳¨׳™׳¡׳•׳ ׳©׳׳–׳”׳” ׳×׳‘׳ ׳™׳× ׳–׳• ׳•׳׳§׳‘׳¢׳× ׳׳× ׳”׳“׳£ ׳‘׳׳§׳•׳׳•.

נ¯ ׳©׳•׳¨׳” ׳×׳—׳×׳•׳ ׳” ׳•׳׳¡׳§׳ ׳” ׳˜׳›׳ ׳™׳×: ׳×׳–׳׳•׳ ׳׳•׳“׳¢ NUMA ׳׳¦׳׳™׳“ ׳×׳”׳׳™׳›׳™׳ ׳׳¦׳•׳׳× ׳”׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳©׳׳”׳ ׳•׳ ׳•׳“׳“ ׳“׳₪׳™ ׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳‘׳”׳×׳׳ ׳׳×׳‘׳ ׳™׳•׳× ׳”׳’׳™׳©׳”, ׳•׳‘׳›׳ ׳׳׳§׳¡׳ ׳׳× ׳”׳’׳™׳©׳” ׳”׳׳§׳•׳׳™׳× ׳”׳׳”׳™׳¨׳”.
׳©׳׳׳” 8.1.4.5: ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳ ׳’׳ ׳™׳‘׳× ׳¢׳‘׳•׳“׳” (Work Stealing) ׳•׳׳™׳׳•׳© ׳×׳•׳¨ ׳“׳•-׳›׳™׳•׳•׳ ׳™ (DEQueue) ג–¼
נ¯ ׳©׳׳׳× ׳”׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳“׳™׳׳׳”: ׳›׳™׳¦׳“ ׳׳×׳׳•׳“׳“ ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳ ׳’׳ ׳™׳‘׳× ׳¢׳‘׳•׳“׳” (Work Stealing) ׳¢׳ ׳—׳•׳¡׳¨ ׳׳™׳–׳•׳ ׳׳§׳¨׳™ ׳‘׳™׳ ׳׳™׳‘׳•׳×, ׳•׳׳“׳•׳¢ ׳©׳™׳׳•׳© ׳‘׳×׳•׳¨ ׳“׳•-׳›׳™׳•׳•׳ ׳™ (Double-Ended Queue / DEQueue) ׳׳׳–׳¢׳¨ ׳׳× ׳×׳—׳¨׳•׳× ׳”׳ ׳¢׳™׳׳” ׳‘׳™׳ ׳”׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳”׳׳§׳•׳׳™ ׳׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳”׳’׳•׳ ׳‘?

נ’¡ ׳¨׳§׳¢ ׳•׳”׳¡׳‘׳¨ ׳׳™׳ ׳˜׳•׳׳™׳˜׳™׳‘׳™: ׳—׳©׳‘׳• ׳¢׳ ׳׳׳₪׳™׳™׳” ׳©׳‘׳” ׳׳›׳ ׳׳•׳₪׳” ׳™׳© ׳©׳•׳׳—׳ ׳¢׳‘׳•׳“׳” ׳¢׳ ׳¢׳¨׳™׳׳× ׳׳’׳©׳™ ׳‘׳¦׳§. ׳”׳׳•׳₪׳” ׳”׳׳§׳•׳׳™ ׳׳•׳§׳— ׳׳’׳©׳™׳ ׳׳”׳—׳׳§ ׳”׳¢׳׳™׳•׳ ׳©׳ ׳”׳¢׳¨׳™׳׳” ׳©׳׳• ׳•׳׳—׳–׳™׳¨ ׳׳•׳×׳ ׳׳©׳ (LIFO - ׳›׳׳• ׳׳—׳¡׳ ׳™׳×, ׳׳” ׳©׳׳ ׳¦׳ ׳–׳™׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳׳˜׳׳•׳ ׳—׳). ׳׳ ׳׳•׳₪׳” ׳©׳›׳ ׳¡׳™׳™׳ ׳׳× ׳›׳ ׳”׳׳’׳©׳™׳ ׳©׳׳• ׳•׳©׳•׳׳—׳ ׳• ׳¨׳™׳§, ׳”׳•׳ ׳׳™׳ ׳• ׳“׳•׳—׳£ ׳׳× ׳™׳“׳™׳• ׳׳¢׳¨׳™׳׳” ׳׳׳׳¢׳׳” ׳•׳׳₪׳¨׳™׳¢ ׳׳׳•׳₪׳” ׳”׳¢׳•׳‘׳“! ׳‘׳׳§׳•׳ ׳–׳׳×, ׳”׳•׳ ׳ ׳™׳’׳© ׳‘׳©׳§׳˜ ׳•׳’׳•׳ ׳‘ ׳׳’׳© ׳׳”׳—׳׳§ ׳”׳×׳—׳×׳•׳ ׳‘׳™׳•׳×׳¨ ׳©׳ ׳”׳¢׳¨׳™׳׳” (FIFO). ׳׳›׳™׳•׳•׳ ׳©׳›׳ ׳׳—׳“ ׳¢׳•׳‘׳“ ׳‘׳§׳¦׳” ׳׳—׳¨, ׳”׳ ׳›׳׳¢׳˜ ׳׳¢׳•׳׳ ׳׳™׳ ׳ ׳׳×׳ ׳’׳©׳™׳ ׳–׳” ׳‘׳–׳”.

נ”¬ ׳ ׳™׳×׳•׳— ׳׳׳’׳•׳¨׳™׳×׳׳™ ׳•׳˜׳›׳ ׳•׳׳•׳’׳™ ׳׳₪׳•׳¨׳˜:
  • ׳¢׳§׳¨׳•׳ ׳”-Work Stealing: ׳׳¢׳‘׳“׳™׳ ׳¢׳׳•׳¡׳™׳ ׳׳׳©׳™׳›׳™׳ ׳׳¢׳‘׳•׳“ ׳¢׳ ׳”׳×׳•׳¨ ׳”׳׳§׳•׳׳™ ׳©׳׳”׳. ׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳¨׳¢׳‘ (׳©׳”׳×׳•׳¨ ׳©׳׳• ׳”׳×׳¨׳•׳§׳) ׳‘׳•׳—׳¨ ׳׳¢׳‘׳“ ׳׳§׳¨׳׳™ ׳•׳׳ ׳¡׳” "׳׳’׳ ׳•׳‘" ׳׳׳ ׳• ׳׳©׳™׳׳•׳× ׳׳•׳›׳ ׳•׳×.
  • ׳׳¨׳›׳™׳˜׳§׳˜׳•׳¨׳× Chase-Lev Work-Stealing DEQueue:
    • ׳”׳‘׳¢׳׳™׳ ׳”׳׳§׳•׳׳™ (Owner): ׳׳‘׳¦׳¢ ׳₪׳¢׳•׳׳•׳× push ׳•-pop ׳׳¨׳׳© ׳”׳×׳•׳¨ (Bottom / Top) ׳›׳׳—׳¡׳ ׳™׳× LIFO ׳׳׳ ׳¦׳•׳¨׳ ׳‘׳©׳•׳ ׳ ׳¢׳™׳׳” (Lock-Free ׳‘׳׳׳¦׳¢׳•׳× ׳¡׳ ׳›׳¨׳•׳ ׳׳§׳•׳׳™ ׳‘׳׳‘׳“).
    • ׳”׳’׳ ׳‘ (Thief): ׳׳‘׳¦׳¢ ׳₪׳¢׳•׳׳× steal ׳׳§׳¦׳”׳• ׳”׳ ׳’׳“׳™ ׳©׳ ׳”׳×׳•׳¨ ׳›׳׳‘׳ ׳” FIFO.
  • ׳™׳×׳¨׳•׳ ׳‘׳™׳¦׳•׳¢׳™׳ ׳›׳₪׳•׳:
